Home Slice Pizza

1415 S Congress Ave, Austin, TX 78704

https://homeslicepizza.com

strong south and central austin visibility for slice and new york style searches, with a sharp fulfillment gap on pizza takeout.

Across 1,249 shared origins in a 10-mile radius around 1415 S Congress Ave, Home Slice Pizza achieves its broadest regional authority on new york style pizza (549 top-3 origins, 1,023 top-10, found at all 1,249 origins) and pizza by the slice (470 top-3 origins, 952 top-10), while broad category discovery (pizza restaurant) reaches top 3 at 21 South/Central origins (261 top-10). By contrast, pizza takeout reaches top 3 at only 6 origins (222 top-10, 216 near-miss ranks 4-10) despite Pizza Takeout being an explicit secondary GBP category and More Home Slice operating next door at 1421 S Congress Ave.

start here

1. Surface explicit takeout and curbside pickup details on the South Congress location page

2. Add New York-style and slice-counter headings to the South Congress location page

3. Account for North Loop sister-location handoff before targeting North Austin origins

important limitations

Search rankings measure organic Google Maps visibility at sampled coordinates; they do not measure walk-in traffic, online ordering conversions, or revenue.

Two queries (new york style pizza and pizza takeout) each had 1 origin out of 1,249 where the provider returned fewer than 30 organic listings after retry; those 2 observations are marked as incomplete absence rather than confirmed top-30 absence.

study objectives and measurement boundaries

objectives

Measure top-3 and top-10 organic Maps visibility across 1,249 shared origins (10-mile radius, 0.5-mile grid spacing) for four distinct customer search themes.

Compare category discovery (pizza restaurant) against style-specific (new york style pizza), format-specific (pizza by the slice), and fulfillment-specific (pizza takeout) searches.

Distinguish genuine visibility drop-offs from Austin sister-location handoffs (More Home Slice at 1421 S Congress Ave and North Loop at 501 E 53rd St).

Identify ranks 4-10 strike zones where targeted website and profile clarity can be evaluated.

evidence boundaries

Measured means found or not returned. Empty, error, and unmeasured origins are excluded. Top-3 and top-10 shares are observed appearances divided by valid measured origins; they are not area or market coverage. A requested depth or returned count alone does not prove absence at a cutoff. Near misses are exactly ranks 4–10 inclusive. Results describe supplied origins and timestamps only; no sampled boundary proves total absence.

Business origin: Home Slice Pizza (1415 S Congress Ave) (user supplied). Coordinates: 30.2492286, -97.7495328.

User-supplied evidence; this renderer does not independently authenticate source labels or origin records. Acquisition verification requires a separate review of the underlying evidence.

reading the findings

Each dot represents a place someone could search from. Its number is the listing position returned for that search, not a customer count. Different searches can reveal different discovery gaps.

For 'pizza restaurant', the listing appeared among the first three results at 21 of 1249 measured locations, and among the first ten at 261.

For 'new york style pizza', the listing appeared among the first three results at 549 of 1249 measured locations, and among the first ten at 1023.

For 'pizza by the slice', the listing appeared among the first three results at 470 of 1249 measured locations, and among the first ten at 952.

For 'pizza takeout', the listing appeared among the first three results at 6 of 1249 measured locations, and among the first ten at 222.

These observations do not establish why rankings differ, how many people searched, or whether a change will generate sales.

why these searches matter

SEARCH 01

pizza restaurant

Find a local pizza restaurant for dining in or ordering a whole pie.

Primary GBP category discovery across Austin dining neighborhoods.

Evidence boundaries

Broad category searches include dine-in, slice shops, and chain pizzerias; national volume is context only.

SEARCH 02

new york style pizza

Find authentic hand-tossed thin-crust New York-style pizza in Austin.

Core culinary identity explicitly stated on the homepage and dominant in customer place topics.

Evidence boundaries

Searchers may type ny pizza or new york style pizza; one representative phrase is selected for the style theme.

SEARCH 03

pizza by the slice

Grab casual individual pizza slices without ordering a full whole pie.

Signature walk-in slice format highlighted on both the website and Google Business Profile.

Evidence boundaries

Includes both dedicated counter-service slice windows (including More Home Slice next door) and full-service pizzerias.

SEARCH 04

pizza takeout

Order whole pies or slices for pickup and takeaway.

Explicit secondary GBP category (Pizza Takeout) and carry-out fulfillment offering on the website.

Evidence boundaries

Takeout intent is more distance-sensitive than destination dining.

where additional measurement would help

Planning review uses a 0.75 km spacing preference, not a universal SEO threshold. These are recommendations, not additional measurements.

Multi-perspective strategy: The initial run dips a toe in the water with a high-resolution neighborhood baseline. Do not be cheap with DataForSEO tokens. A single baseline is step one; almost every business needs an outward enhancement, a regional zoom-out, or both to see the complete competitive picture across multiple zoom levels.

How the findings were checked

Home Slice Pizza operates three distinct Austin Google Business Profiles: 1415 S Congress Ave (CID 12299280573550492680, primary), More Home Slice at 1421 S Congress Ave (CID 8169083705981921284), and 501 E 53rd St (CID 5514358989167054199).

All 4,996 coordinate-query tasks were collected on 2026-10-19 via DataForSEO Google Maps Standard Queue with depth=30, zoom=13, and search_this_area=false (.9976 total scan spend).

what remains unverified

Search rankings measure organic Google Maps visibility at sampled coordinates; they do not measure walk-in traffic, online ordering conversions, or revenue.

Two queries (new york style pizza and pizza takeout) each had 1 origin out of 1,249 where the provider returned fewer than 30 organic listings after retry; those 2 observations are marked as incomplete absence rather than confirmed top-30 absence.
